[5] (Safety - Safety Meeting User Manual_R1_31.05.2026 — Table of Contents (p.2))
1. **MONTHLY SAFETY MEETING** 3
 1.1 **OPENING SCREEN** 4
 1.2 **HOW TO ENTER SAFETY MEETING** 5
 1.3 **HOW TO SELECT FILTERS** 7
 1.4 **HOW TO SEE PENDING ACTIONS** 8
 1.5 **HOW TO OPEN NEW SAFETY MEETING FORM** 9
 1.6 **HOW TO FILL SAFETY MEETING FORM** 10
 1.7 **OFFICE CLOSEOUT FOR SAFETY MEETING FORM** 15
2. **ADDITIONAL SAFETY MEETING** 16
 2.1 **HOW TO OPEN NEW ADDITIONAL SAFETY COMMITTE MEETING (ADD SM)** 17
 2.2 **HOW TO FILL ADD SM FORM** 18

FULL ANSWER:
You can record minutes for:

- A **Monthly Safety Meeting**
- An **Additional Safety Committee Meeting (ADD SM)**

### Monthly Safety Meeting

1. Complete all mandatory fields in the Safety Meeting Form. Enter participant details manually or paste them into the text box.
2. Click **Save** in Part A. This saves the overview and generates a **Report ID**.
3. In **Part B**, review unresolved actions from the previous meeting shown in section **B1.1**.
4. Click **+ Add Action** to add new actions. Enter the nature of the action or select an existing action, then click **Save**.
5. Enter the meeting comments and click **Save**. Mandatory fields marked with **(*)** must be completed.

### Additional Safety Committee Meeting

1. Complete the required information in **Part A – Overview**.
2. Select the applicable **Sub Type**.
3. Enter participant details manually or paste them into the **Participants** text box.
4. Click **Save** to generate the **Report ID**.
5. Complete **Part B** and **Part C** by following the instructions in the Monthly Safety Meeting form.

Office users can add comments and actions and close the meeting in the office-only closeout section.

**Source:**  
- Monthly Safety Meeting User Manual, **1.6 How to Fill Safety Meeting Form**, p. 10; **Part B: Enter Safety Meeting Minutes**, p. 11.  
- Monthly Safety Meeting User Manual, **1.7 Office Closeout for Safety Meeting Form**, p. 15.  
- Monthly Safety Meeting User Manual, **2.2 How to Fill ADD SM Form**, p. 18.

[5] (Safety - Safety Meeting User Manual_R1_31.05.2026 — Table of Contents (p.2))
1. **MONTHLY SAFETY MEETING** 3
 1.1 **OPENING SCREEN** 4
 1.2 **HOW TO ENTER SAFETY MEETING** 5
 1.3 **HOW TO SELECT FILTERS** 7
 1.4 **HOW TO SEE PENDING ACTIONS** 8
 1.5 **HOW TO OPEN NEW SAFETY MEETING FORM** 9
 1.6 **HOW TO FILL SAFETY MEETING FORM** 10
 1.7 **OFFICE CLOSEOUT FOR SAFETY MEETING FORM** 15
2. **ADDITIONAL SAFETY MEETING** 16
 2.1 **HOW TO OPEN NEW ADDITIONAL SAFETY COMMITTE MEETING (ADD SM)** 17
 2.2 **HOW TO FILL ADD SM FORM** 18

FULL ANSWER:
The manual supports these two ways:

**Monthly Safety Meeting**
1. In **Part B – Safety Meeting Minutes**, review unresolved actions from the previous meeting in **section B1.1**.
2. Click **+ Add Action** to add new actions as needed.
3. Enter the comments and click **Save**.
4. Complete all mandatory fields marked with **(*)**.

**Additional Safety Committee Meeting**
1. Complete all required information in **Part A – Overview**.
2. Select the applicable **Sub Type**.
3. Enter the participants manually or paste them into the **Participants** box.
4. Click **Save** to generate the **Report ID**.
5. Complete **Part B and Part C** by following the Monthly Safety Meeting instructions above. **Section D is for office use only**.

Source: Monthly Safety Meeting User Manual, **“PART – B: Enter Safety Meeting Minutes,” p. 11**; **“HOW TO FILL SAFETY MEETING FORM,” p. 10**. Additional Safety Committee Meeting, **“HOW TO FILL ADD SM FORM,” p. 18**.

 1.6 **HOW TO FILL SAFETY MEETING FORM** 10
 1.7 **OFFICE CLOSEOUT FOR SAFETY MEETING FORM** 15
2. **ADDITIONAL SAFETY MEETING** 16
 2.1 **HOW TO OPEN NEW ADDITIONAL SAFETY COMMITTE MEETING (ADD SM)** 17
 2.2 **HOW TO FILL ADD SM FORM** 18

FULL ANSWER:
The manual supports recording minutes in two forms:

**Monthly Safety Meeting**
1. Open **Part B – Safety Meeting Minutes**.
2. Review unresolved actions from the previous meeting in **section B1.1**.
3. Click **“+ Add Action”** to add new actions, if required.
4. Enter or update the comments and click **“Save”**.
5. Complete all mandatory fields marked with **(\*)**.

**Additional Safety Committee Meeting**
1. Complete all required information in **Part A – Overview**.
2. Select the applicable **Sub Type**.
3. Enter the participants.
4. Click **“Save”** to generate the **Report ID**.
5. Complete **Part B and Part C** by following the instructions provided for the Monthly Safety Meeting form. The excerpt does not provide further detailed steps for these parts.

**Office closeout:** Part E of the Monthly Safety Meeting form, and Section D of the Additional Safety Meeting form, are for **office users only**. Office users can add comments and actions and close the meeting.

**Source:** Monthly Safety Meeting User Manual, §1.6 “How to Fill Safety Meeting Form,” p.10; “Part B: Enter Safety Meeting Minutes,” p.11; §1.7 “Office Closeout,” p.15. Additional Safety Committee Meeting, §2.2 “How to Fill ADD SM Form,” p.18.

[5] (Safety - Safety Meeting User Manual_R1_31.05.2026 — Table of Contents (p.2))
1. **MONTHLY SAFETY MEETING** 3
 1.1 **OPENING SCREEN** 4
 1.2 **HOW TO ENTER SAFETY MEETING** 5
 1.3 **HOW TO SELECT FILTERS** 7
 1.4 **HOW TO SEE PENDING ACTIONS** 8
 1.5 **HOW TO OPEN NEW SAFETY MEETING FORM** 9
 1.6 **HOW TO FILL SAFETY MEETING FORM** 10
 1.7 **OFFICE CLOSEOUT FOR SAFETY MEETING FORM** 15
2. **ADDITIONAL SAFETY MEETING** 16
 2.1 **HOW TO OPEN NEW ADDITIONAL SAFETY COMMITTE MEETING (ADD SM)** 17
 2.2 **HOW TO FILL ADD SM FORM** 18

FULL ANSWER:
You can record minutes for:

1. **Monthly Safety Meeting**
2. **Additional Safety Committee Meeting (Add SM)** — Part B and Part C follow the Monthly Safety Meeting procedure.

### Monthly Safety Meeting

1. Complete all mandatory fields in the Safety Meeting Form. Enter participant details manually or paste them into the text box.
2. Click **Save** in Part A. This saves the overview and generates the **Report ID**.
3. In **Part B – Safety Meeting Minutes**, review the unresolved actions from the previous meeting in section **B1.1**.
4. Click **+ Add Action** to add new actions as required. Enter the **Nature of Action**, or select an existing action, then click **Save**.
5. Click **Save** to save the comments. Complete all fields marked with **(*)**.
6. For office closeout, office users may add comments and actions in Part E and close the Monthly Safety Meeting.

### Additional Safety Committee Meeting

1. Complete Part A – Overview, including the applicable **Sub Type** and participant details.
2. Click **Save** to save Part A and generate the **Report ID**.
3. Complete **Part B and Part C** by following the Monthly Safety Meeting instructions above.
4. **Section D is for office use only**; follow the Monthly Safety Meeting Office Closeout procedure.

**Source:**
- Monthly Safety Meeting: *Safety Meeting User Manual_R1_31.05.2026*, sections 1.6, “PART – B: Enter Safety Meeting Minutes,” and 1.7.
- Additional Safety Committee Meeting: *Safety Meeting User Manual_R1_31.05.2026*, section 2.2.

[5] (Safety - Safety Meeting User Manual_R1_31.05.2026 — Table of Contents (p.2))

 1.1 **OPENING SCREEN** 4
 1.2 **HOW TO ENTER SAFETY MEETING** 5
 1.3 **HOW TO SELECT FILTERS** 7
 1.4 **HOW TO SEE PENDING ACTIONS** 8
 1.5 **HOW TO OPEN NEW SAFETY MEETING FORM** 9
 1.6 **HOW TO FILL SAFETY MEETING FORM** 10
 1.7 **OFFICE CLOSEOUT FOR SAFETY MEETING FORM** 15
2. **ADDITIONAL SAFETY MEETING** 16
 2.1 **HOW TO OPEN NEW ADDITIONAL SAFETY COMMITTE MEETING (ADD SM)** 17
 2.2 **HOW TO FILL ADD SM FORM** 18

FULL ANSWER:
To record safety meeting minutes:

1. In the **Monthly Safety Meeting** form, review unresolved actions shown in **section B1.1**.
2. Click **+ Add Action** to add new actions as needed.
3. In the Add Action window, enter the **Nature of Action** or select an existing action, then click **Save**.
4. Complete all mandatory fields marked with **(*)**.
5. Click **Save** to save the comments.

For an **Additional Safety Committee Meeting**, first complete Part A, including the applicable **Sub Type** and participant details, then click **Save** to generate the Report ID. Follow the Monthly Safety Meeting instructions to complete Parts B and C.

**Source:** Monthly Safety Meeting User Manual, §1.6 “How to Fill Safety Meeting Form,” p.10–11; §2.2 “How to Fill ADD SM Form,” p.18.

 2.1 **HOW TO OPEN NEW ADDITIONAL SAFETY COMMITTE MEETING (ADD SM)** 17
 2.2 **HOW TO FILL ADD SM FORM** 18

FULL ANSWER:
You can record minutes in:

- **Monthly Safety Meeting**
- **Additional Safety Committee Meeting**, using the same Part B procedure as the Monthly Safety Meeting

For a **Monthly Safety Meeting**:

1. Complete all required fields in **Part A**.
2. Enter participant details manually or paste them into the **Participants** text box.
3. Click **Save**. This saves Part A and automatically generates the **Report ID**.
4. In **Part B – Safety Meeting Minutes**, review unresolved actions from the previous meeting in **section B1.1**.
5. Click **+ Add Action** to add new actions. Enter the **Nature of Action** or select an existing action, then click **Save**.
6. Enter the comments and click **Save**. Complete all mandatory fields marked with **(*)**.

For an **Additional Safety Committee Meeting**:

1. Complete the required information in **Part A – Overview**.
2. Select the applicable **Sub Type**.
3. Enter participant details.
4. Click **Save** to generate the **Report ID**.
5. Complete **Part B and Part C** by following the Monthly Safety Meeting instructions above.

**Source:** Monthly Safety Meeting User Manual, sections 1.6 “How to Fill Safety Meeting Form” (p.10) and “Part B: Enter Safety Meeting Minutes” (p.11); Additional Safety Meeting, section 2.2 “How to Fill ADD SM Form” (p.18).

 * Component Tree – Hierarchical navigation of equipment categories (1-8). Click to expand; click a component to view details. (Ref Figure 19)
 * Component Details – Full information for the selected component, including Component Information, Running Hours & Condition Monitoring, Jobs, Spares, Maintenance History, Drawings & Manuals, Classification & Regulatory Data, and Requisitions. (Ref Figure 19)

 * <u>Component Tree – Hierarchical navigation of equipment categories (1-8). Click to expand; click a</u> component to view details. (Ref Figure 15)

 * <u>Component Details – Full information for the selected component, including Component</u> <u>Information, Running Hours & Condition Monitoring, Jobs, Spares, Maintenance History,</u> Drawings & Manuals, Classification & Regulatory Data, and Requisitions. (Ref Figure 15)

Figure 93

FULL ANSWER:
The Technical documentation does not specifically cover adding a component in the **Safety module** or placing it directly in the **component tree**. It does cover adding a component in the Office **Components** screen:

1. In the Office Components screen, select the required vessel from the **Vessel** dropdown.
2. Click **Add Component** (or **+ Add Component**).
3. Enter all required component details. All mandatory fields must be completed before saving or submitting.
4. Click **Save** to save the component information.
5. Click **Submit** to add the component to the Components panel.

The excerpt does not state that this procedure adds the component specifically to the Safety module’s component tree.

**Source:** Office Sail Admin manual, §1.1.4.3 “How to Add Components,” p.24.
